       CHAPTER 1
   INTRODUCTION
Section I. GENERAL

1-1. Scope                                             performed must consider available skills, tools,
                                                       test equipment, and time required to perform the
   a. This manual covers maintenance of the            maintenance.
electronic equipment configuration in Army model
AH-1S Helicopter, serial numbers 76-22567                 d. Throughout this manual, the following terms
through 76-22610, 76-22692 through 76-22713, and       are used:
77-22729 through 77-22810. The manual includes
instructions for organizational (AVIM)                       (1) Configuration includes all basic electronic
maintenance personnel to perform periodic              equipment and electronic installation items.
preventive maintenance and_ troubleshooting
procedures for the electronic communications and             (2) Basic electronic equipment denotes
navigational equipment when the equipment is           Government-furnished equipment for which there
installed in the aircraft. This manual also lists      are Department of the Army publications covering
tools and test equipment required by maintenance       detailed maintenance instructions.
personnel to maintain the configuration.
                                                             (3) Electronic installation items denote items
   b. Operating instructions are contained in          furnished by the airframe manufacturer for which
TM55-1520-236-10. Some of the electronic               there are no official publications covering detailed
equipments used in this configuration are covered      maintenance instructions.
